## Overview

Dr. Alexander Ivanov is a psychiatrist in Brooklyn, New York and is affiliated with [Mercy Hospital-Rockville Centre](https://health.usnews.com/best-hospitals/area/ny/mercy-medical-center-6214360). He received his medical degree from Latvian Medical Academy and has been in practice for more than 20 years. Dr. Alexander Ivanov has expertise in treating bipolar disorder, depression, anxiety disorders, among other conditions - see all areas of [expertise](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/alexander-ivanov-585951#expertise). Dr. Alexander Ivanov accepts Cigna - see other [insurance plans accepted](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/alexander-ivanov-585951#insurance). Dr. Alexander Ivanov is highly recommended by patients.

## Specialties

Specialty

### Psychiatry

Psychiatrists diagnose and treat mental illness, such as depression, anxiety disorders, substance abuse, and schizophrenia. Most psychiatrists rely on a mix of medications and psychotherapy.
